1292 — dumbbell one leg fly on exercise ball

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1292 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A dumbbell fly on a stability ball with one leg lifted, which makes balance harder. It works the chest and trains core stability.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on an exercise ball with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing each other. Place one foot on the ground and extend the other leg straight out in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1292.json#/instructions/0, 1292.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Sit on an exercise ball with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing each other.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1292.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Place one foot on the ground and extend the other leg straight out in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1292.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Dumbbell pair, Swiss/stability ball. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1292.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on an exercise ball with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing each other. 1292.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place one foot on the ground and extend the other leg straight out in front of you. 1292.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lean forward slightly and bring your arms out to the sides, keeping a slight bend in your elbows. 1292.json#/instructions/2
  4. Slowly lower the dumbbells down and out to the sides, feeling a stretch in your chest. 1292.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the bottom, then squeeze your chest muscles to bring the dumbbells back up to the starting position. 1292.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch legs and repeat. 1292.json#/instructions/5

1293 — dumbbell press on exercise ball

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1293 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

Press two dumbbells up while your upper back rests on a stability ball. A good way to train the chest with extra core and balance demand.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on an exercise ball with your feet flat on the ground and dumbbells in each hand, resting on your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1293.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Sit on an exercise ball with your feet flat on the ground and dumbbells in each hand, resting on your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1293.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Sit on an exercise ball with your feet flat on the ground and dumbbells in each hand, resting on your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1293.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Dumbbell pair, Swiss/stability ball. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1293.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on an exercise ball with your feet flat on the ground and dumbbells in each hand, resting on your thighs. 1293.json#/instructions/0
  2. Slowly walk your feet forward, rolling the exercise ball until your lower back is supported on the ball and your knees are at a 90-degree angle. 1293.json#/instructions/1
  3. Raise the dumbbells to shoulder height, palms facing forward. 1293.json#/instructions/2
  4. Press the dumbbells upward until your arms are fully extended. 1293.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower the dumbbells back to the starting position. 1293.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1293.json#/instructions/5

1294 — dumbbell pullover hip extension on exercise ball

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1294 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A dumbbell pullover on a stability ball combined with a hip lift. It trains the chest and lats while the glutes and core hold your body steady.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on an exercise ball with your feet flat on the ground and the dumbbell resting on your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1294.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1294.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on an exercise ball with your feet flat on the ground and the dumbbell resting on your thighs. 1294.json#/instructions/0
  2. Slowly walk your feet forward, rolling the exercise ball down your back until your head, neck, and upper back are supported on the ball. 1294.json#/instructions/1
  3. Hold the dumbbell with both hands and extend your arms straight up over your chest, keeping a slight bend in your elbows. 1294.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower the dumbbell behind your head, keeping your arms straight and maintaining control. 1294.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment, then raise the dumbbell back to the starting position. 1294.json#/instructions/4
  6. While keeping your arms extended, lift your hips off the ground, squeezing your glutes and engaging your core. 1294.json#/instructions/5
  7. Lower your hips back down to the starting position. 1294.json#/instructions/6
  8. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1294.json#/instructions/7

1296 — exercise ball pike push up

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1296 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: plank.

A pike push-up with your feet on a stability ball. It builds shoulders and chest and works your core to hold the ball steady.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a high-plank blocking pose on the documented support, with a long aligned torso; preserve any source-defined low-plank or lifted-leg modification.

authored_from_source_context; source verified: false. Body-pose class uses cited setup/action or title/description context, or an explicitly authored neutral default; detailed placement is a reversible proposal. Basis: source_classification. 1296.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Start in a push-up position with your hands on the floor and your shins resting on the stability ball.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1296.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Use toe support under the documented support leg or legs and keep the pelvis level; when a lifted-leg source leaves the first side open, stage the right leg first and then mirror.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 1296.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Swiss/stability ball. For this floor-support pose, stage the documented hand, forearm, foot or knee supports on a level ground plane unless the source names another surface; this is an authored support choice. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1296.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start in a push-up position with your hands on the floor and your shins resting on the stability ball. 1296.json#/instructions/0
  2. Engage your core and lift your hips up towards the ceiling, rolling the ball towards your hands. 1296.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your legs straight and your body in a pike position, forming an inverted V shape. 1296.json#/instructions/2
  4. Bend your elbows and lower your upper body towards the floor, keeping your head in line with your hands. 1296.json#/instructions/3
  5. Push through your hands and extend your arms to return to the starting position. 1296.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1296.json#/instructions/5

1297 — isometric chest squeeze

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1297 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Press your palms or arms together in front of your chest and hold the squeeze. A simple way to feel and train the chest with no equipment.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and your knees slightly bent.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1297.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 1297.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and your knees slightly bent.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1297.json#/instructions/0

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1297.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and your knees slightly bent. 1297.json#/instructions/0
  2. Extend your arms straight out in front of you, parallel to the ground, with your palms facing each other. 1297.json#/instructions/1
  3. Squeeze your chest muscles together as hard as you can, while keeping your arms straight. 1297.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold this position for a few seconds, focusing on contracting your chest muscles. 1297.json#/instructions/3
  5. Release the squeeze and relax your chest muscles. 1297.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1297.json#/instructions/5

1298 — kettlebell one arm floor press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1298 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

Lying on the floor, press a kettlebell straight up with one arm. The floor limits the range, which is gentle on the shoulder while building the chest and triceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie flat on your back on the floor with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground. Hold the kettlebell in one hand with your palm facing towards your feet and your arm extended straight up towards the ceiling.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1298.json#/instructions/0, 1298.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Hold the kettlebell in one hand with your palm facing towards your feet and your arm extended straight up towards the ceiling.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1298.json#/instructions/1

  6. Repeat the push-up motion, dropping down and catching yourself with your hands in the narrower position. 1310.json#/instructions/5

Push a medicine ball forward from your chest, then sprint after it and catch it. It trains explosive chest power plus quick acceleration.

Proposed staging

  6. Continue alternating legs and repeating the exercise for the desired number of repetitions. 1312.json#/instructions/5

Starting Position

Hold the dumbbell with a hammer grip, meaning your palms are facing each other and your fingers are wrapped around the handle.

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1382 after decompression) · Source record

Calf raises with a stability ball held between your back and a wall. It works the calves while the ball helps you stay balanced.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

1391 — sled calf press on leg press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1391 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: source_defined_support.

A calf press done on a leg press sled using only your toes. It builds the calves under a supported load.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Adjust the seat of the leg press machine so that your knees are slightly bent when your feet are on the sled. Place your feet shoulder-width apart on the sled, with your toes pointing forward.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Adjust the seat of the leg press machine so that your knees are slightly bent when your feet are on the sled. 1391.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place your feet shoulder-width apart on the sled, with your toes pointing forward. 1391.json#/instructions/1
  3. Release the safety handles and push the sled away from you by extending your knees and ankles. 1391.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the top of the movement, then slowly lower the sled back down by bending your knees and ankles. 1391.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1391.json#/instructions/4
